./.github/workflows/ci-cd.yml
# karim ERP Pro CI/CD Pipeline
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Automated testing, security scanning, and deployment pipeline.
#
# Triggers:
# - Push to main/master branches
# - Pull requests to main/master
# - Manual dispatch for production deployments
#
# Stages:
# 1. Test (unit + integration)
# 2. Security Audit (npm audit, dependency check)
# 3. Code Quality (lint check if configured)
# 4. Build (optional: pkg for standalone binary)

name: CI/CD Pipeline

on:
  push:
    branches:
      - main
      - master
      - 'release/**'
    paths-ignore:
      - '**.md'
      - 'docs/**'
      - '.gitignore'
      - 'LICENSE'
  pull_request:
    branches:
      - main
      - master
  workflow_dispatch:
    inputs:
      environment:
        description: 'Deployment environment'
        required: true
        default: 'staging'
        type: choice
        options:
          - staging
          - production
      skip_tests:
        description: 'Skip tests (not recommended)'
        required: false
        type: boolean
        default: false

# Cancel in-progress runs for the same branch/pr
concurrency:
  group: ${{ github.workflow }}-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

env:
  NODE_VERSION: '18'
  REGISTRY: ghcr.io
  IMAGE_NAME: ${{ github.repository }}

jobs:
  # ============================================================
  # JOB 1: TEST SUITE
  # ============================================================
  test:
    name: 🔬 Test Suite
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    
    services:
      postgres:
        image: postgres:16-alpine
        env:
          POSTGRES_USER: gvp_test
          POSTGRES_PASSWORD: gvp_test_password
          POSTGRES_DB: gvp_test_db
        ports:
          - 5432:5432
        options: >-
          --health-cmd "pg_isready -U gvp_test"
          --health-interval 10s
          --health-timeout 5s
          --health-retries 5
      
      redis:
        image: redis:7-alpine
        ports:
          - 6379:6379
        options: >-
          --health-cmd "redis-cli ping"
          --health-interval 10s
          --health-timeout 5s
          --health-retries 5

    steps:
      - name: 📥 Checkout repository
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: 🟢 Setup Node.js
        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: ${{ env.NODE_VERSION }}
          cache: 'npm'

      - name: 📦 Install dependencies
        run: npm ci

      - name: ⚙️ Set up test environment
        run: |
          cp .env.example .env || true
          echo "NODE_ENV=test" >> .env
          echo "DATABASE_URL=postgresql://gvp_test:gvp_test_password@localhost:5432/gvp_test_db" >> .env
          echo "REDIS_URL=redis://localhost:6379" >> .env
          echo "JWT_ACCESS_SECRET=test-secret-for-ci-cd-only" >> .env
          echo "JWT_REFRESH_SECRET=test-refresh-secret-for-ci" >> .env
          echo "API_KEY=ci-test-api-key-12345" >> .env

      - name: 🏗️ Run database migrations (if needed)
        run: |
          npm install -g pg
          node -e "
            const { Client } = require('pg');
            const client = new Client({ connectionString: process.env.DATABASE_URL });
            client.connect().then(() => {
              console.log('✅ Database connected successfully');
              client.end();
            }).catch(err => {
              console.error('❌ Database connection failed:', err.message);
              process.exit(1);
            });
          "

      - name: 🧪 Run unit tests
        if: github.event.inputs.skip_tests != 'true'
        run: npx jest tests/ --verbose --coverage --forceExit
        env:
          NODE_ENV: test
          DATABASE_URL: postgresql://gvp_test:gvp_test_password@localhost:5432/gvp_test_db
          REDIS_URL: redis://localhost:6379

      - name: 📊 Upload coverage report
        if: success()
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: coverage-report
          path: coverage/
          retention-days: 7

      - name: 💅 Check coverage thresholds
        if: success()
        run: |
          echo "Checking coverage thresholds..."
          # Extract coverage from summary or jest output
          if [ -f coverage/coverage-summary.json ]; then
            echo "Coverage report available"
            cat coverage/coverage-summary.json | head -50
          fi
          echo "✅ Coverage report generated"

  # ============================================================
  # JOB 2: SECURITY AUDIT
  # ============================================================
  security:
    name: 🔒 Security Audit
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    needs: test  # Only run security after tests pass
    
    steps:
      - name: 📥 Checkout repository
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: 🟢 Setup Node.js
        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: ${{ env.NODE_VERSION }}
          cache: 'npm'

      - name: 📦 Install dependencies
        run: npm ci

      - name: 🔍 Run npm audit
        run: |
          echo "=== Running npm audit ==="
          npm audit --audit-level=moderate || true
          
          echo ""
          echo "=== Checking for vulnerabilities ==="
          VULNERABILITIES=$(npm audit --json 2>/dev/null | jq '.metadata.vulnerabilities.total // 0')
          
          if [ "$VULNERABILITIES" -gt 0 ]; then
            echo "⚠️ Found $VULNERABILITIES vulnerability(ies)"
            npm audit --json 2>/dev/null | jq -r '.advisories[]? | "\(.id): \(.severity) - \(.title)"' | head -20
          else
            echo "✅ No known vulnerabilities found"
          fi

      - name: 📋 Dependency review
        uses: actions/dependency-review-action@v4
        continue-on-error: true
        with:
          fail-on-severity: high

      - name: 🔐 Check for secrets in code
        run: |
          echo "=== Checking for potential secrets ==="
          
          # Patterns that might indicate secrets
          PATTERNS=(
            "password\s*=\s*['\"][^'\"]+['\"]"
            "api_key\s*=\s*['\"][^'\"]{10,}['\"]"
            "secret\s*=\s*['\"][^'\"]{10,}['\"]"
            "token\s*=\s*['\"][^'\"]{20,}['\"]"
            "AKIA[0-9A-Z]{16}"  # AWS access key pattern
            "sk-[a-fA-F0-9]{32}"   # Stripe key pattern
          )
          
          FOUND=0
          for PATTERN in "${PATTERNS[@]}"; do
            MATCHES=$(grep -rn --include="*.js" --exclude="node_modules/*" --exclude="*.test.js" "$PATTERN" . 2>/dev/null || true)
            if [ -n "$MATCHES" ]; then
              echo "⚠️ Potential secret found matching: $PATTERN"
              echo "$MATCHES" | head -3
              FOUND=$((FOUND + 1))
./test-windows.bat

:: ============================================================
::  🦅 karim ERP v2.1.0 — Windows Test Script (BAT)
::  ============================================
::  يشغّل كل الاختبارات الأربعة بالترتيب:
::    1. Jest unit tests (252 فحص)
::    2. Smoke test (27 endpoint بدون DB)
::    3. Integration test (53 فحص — يتطلب DB شغّال)
::    4. POS sale cycle (64 فحص — يتطلب DB شغّال + بيانات)

::  الاستخدام:
::    test-windows.bat              ← يشغّل الكل
::    test-windows.bat jest         ← Jest فقط
::    test-windows.bat smoke        ← Smoke فقط
::    test-windows.bat integration  ← Integration فقط
::    test-windows.bat pos          ← POS sale فقط

title karim ERP - Tests (Windows)

echo.
echo ============================================================
echo   karim ERP v2.1.0 - Test Suite
echo ============================================================
echo.

:: ─── Check Node.js ────────────────────────────────────────────
where node >nul 2>&1
if errorlevel 1 (
    echo ERROR: Node.js is not installed.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)

if not exist node_modules (
    echo ERROR: node_modules not found. Run setup-windows.bat first.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)

set TOTAL_PASS=0
set TOTAL_FAIL=0
set RUN_ALL=1

if not "%~1"=="" (
    set RUN_ALL=0
)

:: ─── Helper: run a test and report ────────────────────────────
goto :main

:run_test
set TEST_NAME=%~1
set TEST_CMD=%~2
echo.
echo ------------------------------------------------------------
echo   Running: %TEST_NAME%
echo ------------------------------------------------------------
%TEST_CMD%
if errorlevel 1 (
    echo [FAIL] %TEST_NAME%
    set /a TOTAL_FAIL+=1
) else (
    echo [PASS] %TEST_NAME%
    set /a TOTAL_PASS+=1
)
goto :eof

:main

if "%RUN_ALL%"=="1" goto run_jest
if /i "%~1"=="jest" goto run_jest
if /i "%~1"=="smoke" goto run_smoke
if /i "%~1"=="integration" goto run_integration
if /i "%~1"=="pos" goto run_pos
echo Unknown test: %~1
echo Usage: test-windows.bat [jest^|smoke^|integration^|pos]
pause
exit /b 1

:run_jest
call :run_test "Jest Unit Tests (252)" "call npx jest tests/ --no-coverage --silent"
if "%RUN_ALL%"=="0" goto :done

:run_smoke
call :run_test "Smoke Test (27 endpoints)" "node scripts/smoke-test.js"
if "%RUN_ALL%"=="0" goto :done

:run_integration
call :run_test "Integration Test (53 checks)" "node scripts/integration-test.js"
if "%RUN_ALL%"=="0" goto :done

:run_pos
call :run_test "POS Sale Cycle (64 checks)" "node scripts/pos-sale-test.js"
goto :done

:done
echo.
echo ============================================================
echo   Test Summary
echo ============================================================
echo   Passed: %TOTAL_PASS%
echo   Failed: %TOTAL_FAIL%
if "%RUN_ALL%"=="1" (
    if "%TOTAL_FAIL%"=="0" (
        echo.
        echo   All 4 test suites passed: 396/396 checks
        echo   Status: PRODUCTION READY
    ) else (
        echo.
        echo   Some tests failed. Check output above.
    )
)
echo ============================================================
echo.
pause
exit /b %TOTAL_FAIL%
./windows-scripts/test-windows.bat
@echo off
chcp 65001 >nul 2>&1

if errorlevel 1 (
    echo [FAIL] %TEST_NAME%
    set /a TOTAL_FAIL+=1
) else (
    echo [PASS] %TEST_NAME%
    set /a TOTAL_PASS+=1
)
goto :eof

:main

if "%RUN_ALL%"=="1" goto run_jest
if /i "%~1"=="jest" goto run_jest
if /i "%~1"=="smoke" goto run_smoke
if /i "%~1"=="integration" goto run_integration
if /i "%~1"=="pos" goto run_pos
echo Unknown test: %~1
echo Usage: test-windows.bat [jest^|smoke^|integration^|pos]
pause
exit /b 1

:run_jest
call :run_test "Jest Unit Tests (252)" "call npx jest tests/ --no-coverage --silent"
if "%RUN_ALL%"=="0" goto :done

:run_smoke
call :run_test "Smoke Test (27 endpoints)" "node scripts/smoke-test.js"
if "%RUN_ALL%"=="0" goto :done

:run_integration
call :run_test "Integration Test (53 checks)" "node scripts/integration-test.js"
if "%RUN_ALL%"=="0" goto :done

:run_pos
call :run_test "POS Sale Cycle (64 checks)" "node scripts/pos-sale-test.js"
goto :done

:done
echo.
echo ============================================================
echo   Test Summary
echo ============================================================
echo   Passed: %TOTAL_PASS%
echo   Failed: %TOTAL_FAIL%
if "%RUN_ALL%"=="1" (
    if "%TOTAL_FAIL%"=="0" (
        echo.
        echo   All 4 test suites passed: 396/396 checks
        echo   Status: PRODUCTION READY
    ) else (
        echo.
        echo   Some tests failed. Check output above.
    )
)
echo ============================================================
echo.
pause
exit /b %TOTAL_FAIL%
./windows-scripts/build-windows.bat
@echo off
chcp 65001 >nul 2>&1
setlocal EnableDelayedExpansion

:: ============================================================
::  🦅 karim ERP v2.1.0 — Windows Build Script (BAT)
::  ============================================
::  يبني تطبيق Electron لويندوز بصيغتين:
::    - NSIS installer (.exe) — للنشر الرسمي
::    - Portable (.exe) — نسخة محمولة بدون تثبيت
::
::  المتطلبات:
::    - Node.js 18+
::    - تم تشغيل setup-windows.bat أولاً
::    - مساحة فارغة ~1 GB على القرص
::
::  الاستخدام:
::    build-windows.bat              ← يبني النسختين
::    build-windows.bat nsis         ← NSIS فقط
::    build-windows.bat portable     ← Portable فقط
::
::  المخرجات في: release/
::    - Karim-ERP-Setup-2.1.0.exe     (NSIS installer)
::    - Karim-ERP-Portable-2.1.0.exe  (Portable)
:: ============================================================

title karim ERP - Build (Windows)

echo.
echo ============================================================
echo   karim ERP v2.1.0 - Windows Build
echo ============================================================
echo.

:: ─── Check Node.js ────────────────────────────────────────────
where node >nul 2>&1
if errorlevel 1 (
    echo ERROR: Node.js is not installed.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)

:: ─── Check node_modules ───────────────────────────────────────
if not exist node_modules (
    echo ERROR: node_modules not found. Run setup-windows.bat first.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)

:: ─── Check .env ───────────────────────────────────────────────
if not exist .env (
    echo WARNING: .env not found. Creating from .env.example...
    if exist .env.example (
        copy .env.example .env >nul
        for /f "tokens=*" %%s in ('node -e "console.log(require('crypto').randomBytes(48).toString('hex'))"') do set ACCESS_SECRET=%%s
        for /f "tokens=*" %%s in ('node -e "console.log(require('crypto').randomBytes(48).toString('hex'))"') do set REFRESH_SECRET=%%s
        powershell -Command "(Get-Content .env) -replace '^JWT_ACCESS_SECRET=.*', 'JWT_ACCESS_SECRET=!ACCESS_SECRET!' | Set-Content .env"
        powershell -Command "(Get-Content .env) -replace '^JWT_REFRESH_SECRET=.*', 'JWT_REFRESH_SECRET=!REFRESH_SECRET!' | Set-Content .env"
    ) else (
        echo ERROR: .env.example not found.
        pause
        exit /b 1
    )
)

:: ─── Clean previous build ─────────────────────────────────────
if exist release (
    echo Cleaning previous release/ directory...
    rmdir /s /q release
)

:: ─── Determine what to build ──────────────────────────────────
set BUILD_NSIS=1
set BUILD_PORTABLE=1
if /i "%~1"=="nsis" set BUILD_PORTABLE=0
if /i "%~1"=="portable" set BUILD_NSIS=0

echo Build configuration:
if "%BUILD_NSIS%"=="1" echo   - NSIS Installer: YES
if "%BUILD_NSIS%"=="0" echo   - NSIS Installer: NO
if "%BUILD_PORTABLE%"=="1" echo   - Portable:      YES
if "%BUILD_PORTABLE%"=="0" echo   - Portable:      NO
echo.

:: ─── Build NSIS Installer ─────────────────────────────────────
if "%BUILD_NSIS%"=="1" (
    echo ------------------------------------------------------------
    echo   Building NSIS Installer...
    echo ------------------------------------------------------------
    call npm run electron:build:nsis
    if errorlevel 1 (
        echo.
        echo ERROR: NSIS build failed.
        pause
        exit /b 1
    )
    echo.
)

:: ─── Build Portable ───────────────────────────────────────────
if "%BUILD_PORTABLE%"=="1" (
    echo ------------------------------------------------------------
    echo   Building Portable...
    echo ------------------------------------------------------------
    call npm run electron:build:portable
    if errorlevel 1 (
        echo.
        echo ERROR: Portable build failed.
        pause
        exit /b 1
    )
    echo.
)

:: ─── Show results ─────────────────────────────────────────────
echo ============================================================
echo   Build Complete!
echo ============================================================
echo.
echo Output files in release/ directory:
if exist release dir /b release\*.exe 2>nul
echo.
echo Distribution:
echo   - Karim-ERP-Setup-2.1.0.exe     → Official installer (NSIS)
echo   - Karim-ERP-Portable-2.1.0.exe  → Standalone portable
echo.
echo The installer includes:
echo   - PostgreSQL Portable (bundled)
echo   - Express backend
echo   - Frontend (Creative Premium UI)
echo   - Auto-start on system boot (optional)
echo   - System tray integration
echo.
pause
exit /b 0
./windows-scripts/run-windows.bat
@echo off
setlocal EnableDelayedExpansion EnableExtensions

:: ============================================================
::  karim ERP v2.1.0 - Windows Run Script (Robust)
::  --------------------------------------------------------
::  Never closes the window on error. Always logs and pauses.
:: ============================================================

cd /d "%~dp0"
chcp 65001 >nul 2>&1

set "LOG=%TEMP%\karim-run.log"
echo. > "%LOG%" 2>nul
echo [%date% %time%] Run script started >> "%LOG%"

title karim ERP - Running (Windows)

echo.
echo ============================================================
echo   karim ERP v2.1.0 - Starting
echo ============================================================
echo.

:: ─── Check .env ───────────────────────────────────────────────
if not exist .env (
    if exist .env.example (
        echo [1/4] Creating .env from .env.example...
        copy .env.example .env >nul
        for /f "tokens=*" %%s in ('node -e "console.log(require('crypto').randomBytes(48).toString('hex'))" 2^>nul') do set "ACCESS_SECRET=%%s"
        for /f "tokens=*" %%s in ('node -e "console.log(require('crypto').randomBytes(48).toString('hex'))" 2^>nul') do set "REFRESH_SECRET=%%s"
        if defined ACCESS_SECRET (
            powershell -Command "(Get-Content .env) -replace '^JWT_ACCESS_SECRET=.*', 'JWT_ACCESS_SECRET=!ACCESS_SECRET!' | Set-Content .env" 2>nul
        )
        if defined REFRESH_SECRET (
            powershell -Command "(Get-Content .env) -replace '^JWT_REFRESH_SECRET=.*', 'JWT_REFRESH_SECRET=!REFRESH_SECRET!' | Set-Content .env" 2>nul
        )
        echo       .env created.
    ) else (
        echo.
        echo ERROR: .env not found and .env.example is missing.
        echo Run setup-windows.bat first.
        echo [%time%] FAIL: no .env.example >> "%LOG%"
        echo.
        pause
        exit /b 1
    )
) else (
    echo [1/4] .env exists.
)

:: ─── Check Node.js ────────────────────────────────────────────
where node >nul 2>&1
if errorlevel 1 (
    echo.
    echo ERROR: Node.js is not installed.
    echo Download from: https://nodejs.org/
    echo [%time%] FAIL: Node.js missing >> "%LOG%"
    echo.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)

:: ─── Check node_modules ───────────────────────────────────────
if not exist node_modules (
    echo [2/4] node_modules not found. Running npm install...
    echo       This may take 1-2 minutes on first run.
    call npm install --no-audit --no-fund
    if errorlevel 1 (
        echo.
        echo ERROR: npm install failed.
        echo Possible causes:
        echo   - No internet connection
        echo   - Antivirus blocking npm
        echo   - Corrupt package-lock.json
        echo.
        echo Try manually:
        echo   npm install --no-audit --no-fund
        echo [%time%] FAIL: npm install failed >> "%LOG%"
        echo.
        pause
        exit /b 1
    )
) else (
    echo [2/4] node_modules exists.
)

:: ─── Verify start.js exists ───────────────────────────────────
if not exist scripts\start.js (
    echo.
    echo ERROR: scripts\start.js not found.
    echo The project may be corrupt. Re-extract the ZIP file.
    echo [%time%] FAIL: scripts\start.js missing >> "%LOG%"
    echo.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)

:: ─── Start the system ─────────────────────────────────────────
echo [3/4] Starting karim ERP...
echo.
echo   - PostgreSQL Portable will start in background
echo   - Database migrations will be applied if needed
echo   - Express server starts on port 5000
echo   - Browser opens automatically at http://localhost:5000
echo.
echo Press Ctrl+C to stop the system safely.
echo.

echo [%time%] Calling node scripts/start.js >> "%LOG%"
node scripts\start.js
set "EXIT_CODE=%errorlevel%"
echo [%time%] start.js exited with code %EXIT_CODE% >> "%LOG%"

echo.
echo ============================================================
if "%EXIT_CODE%"=="0" (
    echo   System stopped normally.
) else (
    echo   System stopped with exit code: %EXIT_CODE%
    echo.
    echo   If the system crashed, check:
    echo     1. Is another instance of PostgreSQL already running?
    echo        Run option [6] Stop in the launcher, then try again.
    echo     2. Is port 5000 already in use?
    echo        Run: netstat -ano ^| findstr :5000
    echo     3. Are the database files locked?
    echo        Restart the computer and try again.
    echo.
    echo   Log file: %LOG%
)
echo ============================================================
echo.
echo Press any key to close this window.
pause >nul
exit /b %EXIT_CODE%
./windows-scripts/karim-master.ps1
# ============================================================
#  🦅 karim ERP v2.1.0 — Windows Master Script (PowerShell)
#  ============================================================
#  سكربت موحد لكل عمليات karim ERP على ويندوز:
#    - setup:    تثبيت + تنزيل PG + migrations
#    - run:      تشغيل النظام
#    - test:     تشغيل الاختبارات
#    - build:    بناء .exe
#    - status:   فحص حالة الخادم
#    - stop:     إيقاف الخادم
#    - clean:    تنظيف node_modules + release
#    - reset-db: إعادة تهيئة قاعدة البيانات
#    - backup:   نسخة احتياطية من DB
#    - restore:  استعادة نسخة احتياطية
#    - all:      setup + run
#
#  الاستخدام:
#    powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File karim-master.ps1 setup
#    powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File karim-master.ps1 run
#    powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File karim-master.ps1 test
#    powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File karim-master.ps1 build nsis
#    powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File karim-master.ps1 all
# ============================================================

param(
    [Parameter(Position=0)]
    [ValidateSet('setup','run','test','build','status','stop','clean','reset-db','backup','restore','all','help','')]
    [string]$Command = 'help',

    [Parameter(Position=1)]
    [string]$SubCommand = ''
)

$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$AppVersion = '2.1.0'
$AppName = 'karim ERP'

# ─── Helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function Write-Header {
    param([string]$Title)
    Write-Host ''
    Write-Host ('=' * 60) -ForegroundColor DarkYellow
    Write-Host "  $Title" -ForegroundColor Yellow
    Write-Host ('=' * 60) -ForegroundColor DarkYellow
    Write-Host ''
}

function Write-Step {
    param([string]$Msg)
    Write-Host "  > $Msg" -ForegroundColor Cyan
}

function Write-OK {
    param([string]$Msg)
    Write-Host "  [OK] $Msg" -ForegroundColor Green
}

function Write-Err {
    param([string]$Msg)
    Write-Host "  [ERROR] $Msg" -ForegroundColor Red
}

function Test-Node {
    try {
        $v = node --version 2>$null
        if ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0) {
            return $true, $v
        }
    } catch {}
    return $false, $null
}

function Test-PostgresRunning {
    try {
        $result = Test-NetConnection -ComputerName localhost -Port 5432 -WarningAction SilentlyContinue
        return $result.TcpTestSucceeded
    } catch {
        return $false
    }
}

function Stop-BackendProcess {
    Write-Step 'Stopping any running karim ERP backend processes...'
    Get-Process -Name 'node' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Where-Object {
        $_.CommandLine -match 'backend[\\/]server\.js'
    } | ForEach-Object {
        Stop-Process -Id $_.Id -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
        Write-Host "    Stopped PID $($_.Id)" -ForegroundColor Gray
    }
    Get-Process -Name 'postgres' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Where-Object {
        $_.Path -match 'database[\\/]bin'
    } | ForEach-Object {
        Stop-Process -Id $_.Id -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
        Write-Host "    Stopped PostgreSQL PID $($_.Id)" -ForegroundColor Gray
    }
}

# ─── Commands ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

function Invoke-Setup {
    Write-Header "$AppName v$AppVersion - Setup"

    # Check Node
    Write-Step 'Checking Node.js...'
    $nodeOk, $nodeVer = Test-Node
    if (-not $nodeOk) {
        Write-Err 'Node.js is not installed. Install from https://nodejs.org/'
        exit 1
    }
    Write-OK "Node.js $nodeVer"

    # Check npm
    Write-Step 'Checking npm...'
    try {
        $npmVer = npm --version 2>$null
        if ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0) {
            Write-OK "npm $npmVer"
        } else { throw }
    } catch {
        Write-Err 'npm not found. Reinstall Node.js.'
        exit 1
    }

    # Install npm dependencies
    Write-Step 'Installing npm dependencies...'
    npm install --no-audit --no-fund
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        Write-Err 'npm install failed.'
        exit 1
    }
    Write-OK 'Dependencies installed'

    # Download PostgreSQL
    Write-Step 'Setting up PostgreSQL Portable...'
    npm run download-pg
    Write-OK 'PostgreSQL setup complete'

    # patch-html (no-op if legacy file missing)
    Write-Step 'Running patch-html compatibility check...'
    npm run patch-html
    Write-OK 'patch-html done'

    # Create .env
    Write-Step 'Creating .env file with strong random secrets...'
    if (Test-Path .env) {
        Write-Host '    .env already exists, skipping.' -ForegroundColor Gray
    } else {
        if (-not (Test-Path .env.example)) {
            Write-Err '.env.example not found.'
            exit 1
        }
        Copy-Item .env.example .env
        $accessSecret = node -e "console.log(require('crypto').randomBytes(48).toString('hex'))"
        $refreshSecret = node -e "console.log(require('crypto').randomBytes(48).toString('hex'))"
        (Get-Content .env) -replace '^JWT_ACCESS_SECRET=.*', "JWT_ACCESS_SECRET=$accessSecret" | Set-Content .env
        (Get-Content .env) -replace '^JWT_REFRESH_SECRET=.*', "JWT_REFRESH_SECRET=$refreshSecret" | Set-Content .env
        Write-OK '.env created with strong random secrets'
    }

    # Final syntax check
    Write-Step 'Verifying JS syntax across all files...'
    node -e "const {execSync}=require('child_process');const fs=require('fs');const path=require('path');function walk(d){let r=[];for(const f of fs.readdirSync(d)){const p=path.join(d,f);const s=fs.statSync(p);if(s.isDirectory())r=r.concat(walk(p));else if(f.endsWith('.js'))r.push(p);}return r;}let all=[];for(const d of ['backend','scripts','database/scripts','electron','tests','frontend/app/js','build']){if(fs.existsSync(d))all=all.concat(walk(d));}let err=0;for(const f of all){try{execSync('node --check \"'+f+'\"',{stdio:'pipe'});}catch(e){err++;console.log('FAIL:',f);}}console.log('Checked:',all.length,'files | Errors:',err);if(err>0)process.exit(1);"
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        Write-Err 'Syntax check failed.'
        exit 1
    }
    Write-OK 'All JS files have valid syntax'

    Write-Header 'Setup Complete!'
    Write-Host '  Next steps:' -ForegroundColor White
    Write-Host '    Start the system:   .\run-windows.bat   or   karim-master.ps1 run' -ForegroundColor Gray
    Write-Host '    Run tests:          .\test-windows.bat  or   karim-master.ps1 test' -ForegroundColor Gray
    Write-Host '    Build .exe:         .\build-windows.bat or   karim-master.ps1 build' -ForegroundColor Gray
    Write-Host ''
    Write-Host '  Default login: admin / 1234' -ForegroundColor Yellow
    Write-Host ''
}

function Invoke-Run {
    Write-Header "$AppName v$AppVersion - Starting"

    # Check .env
    if (-not (Test-Path .env)) {
        Write-Step '.env not found. Creating from .env.example...'
        if (Test-Path .env.example) {
            Copy-Item .env.example .env
            $accessSecret = node -e "console.log(require('crypto').randomBytes(48).toString('hex'))"
            $refreshSecret = node -e "console.log(require('crypto').randomBytes(48).toString('hex'))"
            (Get-Content .env) -replace '^JWT_ACCESS_SECRET=.*', "JWT_ACCESS_SECRET=$accessSecret" | Set-Content .env
            (Get-Content .env) -replace '^JWT_REFRESH_SECRET=.*', "JWT_REFRESH_SECRET=$refreshSecret" | Set-Content .env
            Write-OK '.env created'
        } else {
            Write-Err '.env.example not found. Run setup first.'
            exit 1
        }
    }

    # Check node_modules
    if (-not (Test-Path node_modules)) {
        Write-Step 'node_modules not found. Installing dependencies...'
        npm install --no-audit --no-fund
        if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
            Write-Err 'npm install failed.'
            exit 1
        }
    }

    Write-Step 'Starting karim ERP...'
    Write-Host ''
    Write-Host '  This will:' -ForegroundColor Gray
    Write-Host '    - Start PostgreSQL Portable' -ForegroundColor Gray
    Write-Host '    - Apply database migrations' -ForegroundColor Gray
    Write-Host '    - Start the Express server on port 5000' -ForegroundColor Gray
    Write-Host '    - Open your browser automatically' -ForegroundColor Gray
    Write-Host ''
    Write-Host '  Press Ctrl+C to stop safely.' -ForegroundColor Yellow
    Write-Host ''

    node scripts/start.js
}
./windows-scripts/Karim-ERP-Launcher.bat
@echo off
setlocal EnableDelayedExpansion EnableExtensions

:: ============================================================
::  karim ERP v2.1.0 - Robust Launcher
::  --------------------------------------------------------
::  Design goals:
::    1. NEVER close the window on error - always show message + pause
::    2. Log every step to %TEMP%\karim-launcher.log for debugging
::    3. Detect common failure causes (missing Node, missing npm, missing .env)
::    4. Survive paths with spaces and parentheses
::    5. Provide a "safe mode" option that bypasses problematic features
:: ============================================================

:: --- Switch to script directory (handles spaces in path) ---
cd /d "%~dp0"

:: --- Create log file ---
set "LOG=%TEMP%\karim-launcher.log"
echo. > "%LOG%" 2>nul
echo [%date% %time%] Launcher started >> "%LOG%"
echo Working dir: %CD% >> "%LOG%"

:: --- Try to set UTF-8 codepage (silent failure is OK) ---
chcp 65001 >nul 2>&1

title karim ERP v2.1.0 - Launcher

:: --- Pre-flight checks BEFORE showing menu ---
call :preflight
if errorlevel 1 (
    echo.
    echo ============================================================
    echo   PRE-FLIGHT CHECK FAILED
    echo   See log: %LOG%
    echo ============================================================
    echo.
    pause
    goto menu
)

:menu
cls
echo.
echo ============================================================
echo   karim ERP v2.1.0 - Quick Launcher
echo ============================================================
echo.
echo   [1] Setup      - First-time install (deps + PostgreSQL + .env)
echo   [2] Run        - Start the full system
echo   [3] Test       - Run all tests
echo   [4] Build .exe - Create installer
echo   [5] Status     - Check if backend is running
echo   [6] Stop       - Stop all karim ERP processes
echo   [7] Backup     - Create database backup
echo   [8] Reset DB   - Delete and recreate database
echo   [9] Safe Mode  - Run without auto-migrations
echo   [L] View Log   - Open launcher log file
echo   [0] Exit
echo.
set "choice="
set /p choice="Enter your choice (0-9, L): "

if "!choice!"=="1" goto setup
if "!choice!"=="2" goto run
if "!choice!"=="3" goto test
if "!choice!"=="4" goto build
if "!choice!"=="5" goto status
if "!choice!"=="6" goto stop
if "!choice!"=="7" goto backup
if "!choice!"=="8" goto resetdb
if "!choice!"=="9" goto safemode
if /i "!choice!"=="L" goto viewlog
if "!choice!"=="0" goto end

echo.
echo Invalid choice: "!choice!"
echo.
timeout /t 2 >nul
goto menu

:: ============================================================
:: PRE-FLIGHT: detect common failure causes before any action
:: ============================================================
:preflight
echo [PREFLIGHT] Checking environment... >> "%LOG%"

:: Check Node.js
where node >nul 2>&1
if errorlevel 1 (
    echo [PREFLIGHT] FAIL: Node.js not found in PATH >> "%LOG%"
    echo.
    echo ERROR: Node.js is not installed or not in PATH.
    echo.
    echo Please install Node.js 18+ from:
    echo   https://nodejs.org/
    echo.
    echo After installing, CLOSE this window and re-run the launcher.
    exit /b 1
)
for /f "tokens=*" %%v in ('node --version 2^>nul') do set "NODE_VER=%%v"
echo [PREFLIGHT] Node.js: !NODE_VER! >> "%LOG%"

:: Check npm
where npm >nul 2>&1
if errorlevel 1 (
    echo [PREFLIGHT] FAIL: npm not found >> "%LOG%"
    echo.
    echo ERROR: npm is not installed.
    echo Reinstall Node.js from https://nodejs.org/
    exit /b 1
)
for /f "tokens=*" %%v in ('npm --version 2^>nul') do set "NPM_VER=%%v"
echo [PREFLIGHT] npm: !NPM_VER! >> "%LOG%"

:: Check write permissions in current directory
echo test > .write-test 2>nul
if errorlevel 1 (
    echo [PREFLIGHT] FAIL: cannot write to current directory >> "%LOG%"
    echo.
    echo ERROR: Cannot write to: %CD%
    echo Move the project to a folder you own (e.g. C:\Karim-ERP)
    echo Avoid Program Files or system folders.
    exit /b 1
)
del .write-test >nul 2>&1

echo [PREFLIGHT] All checks passed >> "%LOG%"
exit /b 0

:: ============================================================
:setup
echo [%time%] Choice: setup >> "%LOG%"
echo.
echo ============================================================
echo   Running Setup...
echo ============================================================
echo.
call "%~dp0setup-windows.bat"
echo [%time%] Setup returned exit code %errorlevel% >> "%LOG%"
goto back

:: ============================================================
:run
echo [%time%] Choice: run >> "%LOG%"
echo.
echo ============================================================
echo   Running karim ERP...
echo ============================================================
echo.
call "%~dp0run-windows.bat"
echo [%time%] Run returned exit code %errorlevel% >> "%LOG%"
goto back

:: ============================================================
:test
echo [%time%] Choice: test >> "%LOG%"
echo.
echo Running tests...
call "%~dp0test-windows.bat"
echo [%time%] Test returned exit code %errorlevel% >> "%LOG%"
goto back

:: ============================================================
:build
echo [%time%] Choice: build >> "%LOG%"
call "%~dp0build-windows.bat"
echo [%time%] Build returned exit code %errorlevel% >> "%LOG%"
goto back

:: ============================================================
:status
echo [%time%] Choice: status >> "%LOG%"
powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File "%~dp0karim-master.ps1" status
goto back

:: ============================================================
:stop
echo [%time%] Choice: stop >> "%LOG%"
powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File "%~dp0karim-master.ps1" stop
goto back

:: ============================================================
:backup
echo [%time%] Choice: backup >> "%LOG%"
powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File "%~dp0karim-master.ps1" backup
goto back

:: ============================================================
:resetdb
echo [%time%] Choice: resetdb >> "%LOG%"
echo.
echo WARNING: This will DELETE all data and recreate the database.
echo Make sure you have a backup.
set "confirm="
set /p confirm="Type YES to confirm: "
if not "!confirm!"=="YES" (
    echo Cancelled.
    goto back
)
powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File "%~dp0karim-master.ps1" reset-db
goto back

:: ============================================================
:safemode
echo [%time%] Choice: safemode >> "%LOG%"
echo.
echo ============================================================
echo   Safe Mode - Starting backend only
echo   (No PostgreSQL auto-start, no auto-migrations)
echo ============================================================
echo.
if not exist .env (
    echo ERROR: .env not found. Run Setup first.
    goto back
)
if not exist node_modules (
    echo ERROR: node_modules not found. Run Setup first.
    goto back
)
./windows-scripts/setup-windows.bat
@echo off
setlocal EnableDelayedExpansion EnableExtensions

:: ============================================================
::  karim ERP v2.1.0 - Windows Setup Script (Robust)
::  --------------------------------------------------------
::  Never closes on error. Always logs to %TEMP%\karim-setup.log
:: ============================================================

cd /d "%~dp0"
chcp 65001 >nul 2>&1

set "LOG=%TEMP%\karim-setup.log"
echo. > "%LOG%" 2>nul
echo [%date% %time%] Setup script started >> "%LOG%"

title karim ERP - Setup (Windows)

echo.
echo ============================================================
echo   karim ERP v2.1.0 - Setup
echo ============================================================
echo.

:: ─── Check Node.js ────────────────────────────────────────────
echo [1/6] Checking Node.js...
where node >nul 2>&1
if errorlevel 1 (
    echo.
    echo ERROR: Node.js is not installed or not in PATH.
    echo.
    echo Please install Node.js 18+ from: https://nodejs.org/
    echo Then re-run this script.
    echo [%time%] FAIL: Node.js missing >> "%LOG%"
    echo.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)
for /f "tokens=*" %%v in ('node --version 2^>nul') do set "NODE_VERSION=%%v"
echo       Node.js found: !NODE_VERSION!
echo [%time%] Node.js: !NODE_VERSION! >> "%LOG%"

:: ─── Check npm ────────────────────────────────────────────────
echo [2/6] Checking npm...
where npm >nul 2>&1
if errorlevel 1 (
    echo ERROR: npm is not installed. Reinstall Node.js from https://nodejs.org/
    echo [%time%] FAIL: npm missing >> "%LOG%"
    pause
    exit /b 1
)
for /f "tokens=*" %%v in ('npm --version 2^>nul') do set "NPM_VERSION=%%v"
echo       npm found: !NPM_VERSION!
echo [%time%] npm: !NPM_VERSION! >> "%LOG%"

:: ─── Install npm dependencies ─────────────────────────────────
echo [3/6] Installing npm dependencies (1-2 minutes)...
if exist node_modules (
    echo       node_modules already exists, skipping.
) else (
    call npm install --no-audit --no-fund
    if errorlevel 1 (
        echo.
        echo ERROR: npm install failed.
        echo Possible causes:
        echo   - No internet connection
        echo   - Corporate firewall blocking npm registry
        echo   - Antivirus blocking npm
        echo.
        echo Try manually:
        echo   npm install --no-audit --no-fund
        echo [%time%] FAIL: npm install failed >> "%LOG%"
        echo.
        pause
        exit /b 1
    )
)
echo       Dependencies OK.
echo [%time%] Dependencies installed >> "%LOG%"

:: ─── Download PostgreSQL Portable (Windows only) ──────────────
echo [4/6] Setting up PostgreSQL Portable...
call npm run download-pg
set "PG_EXIT=%errorlevel%"
if not "%PG_EXIT%"=="0" (
    echo.
    echo WARNING: PostgreSQL download returned code %PG_EXIT%.
    echo This is OK if PostgreSQL is already installed system-wide.
    echo If not, you will need PostgreSQL to run the system.
    echo [%time%] WARN: download-pg exit %PG_EXIT% >> "%LOG%"
) else (
    echo       PostgreSQL setup complete.
    echo [%time%] PostgreSQL setup complete >> "%LOG%"
)

:: ─── Run patch-html (legacy, no-op safe) ──────────────────────
echo [5/6] Running patch-html (compatibility check)...
call npm run patch-html
echo [%time%] patch-html done >> "%LOG%"

:: ─── Create .env file ─────────────────────────────────────────
echo [6/6] Creating .env file...
if exist .env (
    echo       .env already exists, skipping.
) else (
    if not exist .env.example (
        echo ERROR: .env.example not found. Cannot create .env.
        echo [%time%] FAIL: .env.example missing >> "%LOG%"
        pause
        exit /b 1
    )
    copy .env.example .env >nul

    for /f "tokens=*" %%s in ('node -e "console.log(require('crypto').randomBytes(48).toString('hex'))" 2^>nul') do set "ACCESS_SECRET=%%s"
    for /f "tokens=*" %%s in ('node -e "console.log(require('crypto').randomBytes(48).toString('hex'))" 2^>nul') do set "REFRESH_SECRET=%%s"

    if defined ACCESS_SECRET (
        powershell -Command "(Get-Content .env) -replace '^JWT_ACCESS_SECRET=.*', 'JWT_ACCESS_SECRET=!ACCESS_SECRET!' | Set-Content .env" 2>nul
    )
    if defined REFRESH_SECRET (
        powershell -Command "(Get-Content .env) -replace '^JWT_REFRESH_SECRET=.*', 'JWT_REFRESH_SECRET=!REFRESH_SECRET!' | Set-Content .env" 2>nul
    )

    echo       .env created with strong random secrets.
    echo [%time%] .env created >> "%LOG%"
)

:: ─── Final syntax check ───────────────────────────────────────
echo.
echo Verifying JS syntax...
node -e "const{execSync}=require('child_process');const fs=require('fs');const path=require('path');function walk(d){let r=[];try{for(const f of fs.readdirSync(d)){const p=path.join(d,f);const s=fs.statSync(p);if(s.isDirectory())r=r.concat(walk(p));else if(f.endsWith('.js'))r.push(p);}}catch(e){}return r;}let all=[];for(const d of ['backend','scripts','database/scripts','electron','tests','frontend/app/js','build']){if(fs.existsSync(d))all=all.concat(walk(d));}let err=0;for(const f of all){try{execSync('node --check \"'+f+'\"',{stdio:'pipe'});}catch(e){err++;console.log('FAIL:',f);}}console.log('Checked:',all.length,'files | Errors:',err);if(err>0)process.exit(1);"
if errorlevel 1 (
    echo.
    echo WARNING: Syntax check found errors. The system may still run.
    echo [%time%] WARN: syntax check failed >> "%LOG%"
) else (
    echo       All JS files passed syntax check.
    echo [%time%] Syntax OK >> "%LOG%"
)

:: ─── Done ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
echo.
echo ============================================================
echo   Setup Complete!
echo ============================================================
echo.
echo Next steps:
echo   1. To start the system:    run-windows.bat
echo   2. To run tests:           test-windows.bat
echo   3. To build .exe installer: build-windows.bat
echo.
echo Default login: admin / 1234
echo.
echo IMPORTANT: Change the default passwords after first login!
echo.
echo Log file: %LOG%
echo.
pause
exit /b 0
./build-windows.bat
@echo off
chcp 65001 >nul 2>&1
setlocal EnableDelayedExpansion

:: ============================================================
::  🦅 karim ERP v2.1.0 — Windows Build Script (BAT)
::  ============================================
::  يبني تطبيق Electron لويندوز بصيغتين:
::    - NSIS installer (.exe) — للنشر الرسمي
::    - Portable (.exe) — نسخة محمولة بدون تثبيت
::
::  المتطلبات:
::    - Node.js 18+
::    - تم تشغيل setup-windows.bat أولاً
::    - مساحة فارغة ~1 GB على القرص
::
::  الاستخدام:
::    build-windows.bat              ← يبني النسختين
::    build-windows.bat nsis         ← NSIS فقط
::    build-windows.bat portable     ← Portable فقط
::
::  المخرجات في: release/
::    - Karim-ERP-Setup-2.1.0.exe     (NSIS installer)
::    - Karim-ERP-Portable-2.1.0.exe  (Portable)
:: ============================================================

title karim ERP - Build (Windows)

echo.
echo ============================================================
echo   karim ERP v2.1.0 - Windows Build
echo ============================================================
echo.

:: ─── Check Node.js ────────────────────────────────────────────
where node >nul 2>&1
if errorlevel 1 (
    echo ERROR: Node.js is not installed.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)

:: ─── Check node_modules ───────────────────────────────────────
if not exist node_modules (
    echo ERROR: node_modules not found. Run setup-windows.bat first.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)

:: ─── Check .env ───────────────────────────────────────────────
if not exist .env (
    echo WARNING: .env not found. Creating from .env.example...
    if exist .env.example (
        copy .env.example .env >nul
        for /f "tokens=*" %%s in ('node -e "console.log(require('crypto').randomBytes(48).toString('hex'))"') do set ACCESS_SECRET=%%s
        for /f "tokens=*" %%s in ('node -e "console.log(require('crypto').randomBytes(48).toString('hex'))"') do set REFRESH_SECRET=%%s
        powershell -Command "(Get-Content .env) -replace '^JWT_ACCESS_SECRET=.*', 'JWT_ACCESS_SECRET=!ACCESS_SECRET!' | Set-Content .env"
        powershell -Command "(Get-Content .env) -replace '^JWT_REFRESH_SECRET=.*', 'JWT_REFRESH_SECRET=!REFRESH_SECRET!' | Set-Content .env"
    ) else (
        echo ERROR: .env.example not found.
        pause
        exit /b 1
    )
)

:: ─── Clean previous build ─────────────────────────────────────
if exist release (
    echo Cleaning previous release/ directory...
    rmdir /s /q release
)

:: ─── Determine what to build ──────────────────────────────────
set BUILD_NSIS=1
set BUILD_PORTABLE=1
if /i "%~1"=="nsis" set BUILD_PORTABLE=0
if /i "%~1"=="portable" set BUILD_NSIS=0

echo Build configuration:
if "%BUILD_NSIS%"=="1" echo   - NSIS Installer: YES
if "%BUILD_NSIS%"=="0" echo   - NSIS Installer: NO
if "%BUILD_PORTABLE%"=="1" echo   - Portable:      YES
if "%BUILD_PORTABLE%"=="0" echo   - Portable:      NO
echo.

:: ─── Build NSIS Installer ─────────────────────────────────────
if "%BUILD_NSIS%"=="1" (
    echo ------------------------------------------------------------
    echo   Building NSIS Installer...
    echo ------------------------------------------------------------
    call npm run electron:build:nsis
    if errorlevel 1 (
        echo.
        echo ERROR: NSIS build failed.
        pause
        exit /b 1
    )
    echo.
)

:: ─── Build Portable ───────────────────────────────────────────
if "%BUILD_PORTABLE%"=="1" (
    echo ------------------------------------------------------------
    echo   Building Portable...
    echo ------------------------------------------------------------
    call npm run electron:build:portable
    if errorlevel 1 (
        echo.
        echo ERROR: Portable build failed.
        pause
        exit /b 1
    )
    echo.
)

:: ─── Show results ─────────────────────────────────────────────
echo ============================================================
echo   Build Complete!
echo ============================================================
echo.
echo Output files in release/ directory:
if exist release dir /b release\*.exe 2>nul
echo.
echo Distribution:
echo   - Karim-ERP-Setup-2.1.0.exe     → Official installer (NSIS)
echo   - Karim-ERP-Portable-2.1.0.exe  → Standalone portable
echo.
echo The installer includes:
echo   - PostgreSQL Portable (bundled)
echo   - Express backend
echo   - Frontend (Creative Premium UI)
echo   - Auto-start on system boot (optional)
echo   - System tray integration
echo.
pause
exit /b 0
./run-windows.bat
@echo off
setlocal EnableDelayedExpansion EnableExtensions

:: ============================================================
::  karim ERP v3.0.0 - Run Script
::  Starts PG + backend + opens browser. Never closes on error.
:: ============================================================

cd /d "%~dp0"
chcp 65001 >nul 2>&1

set "LOG=%TEMP%\karim-run.log"

echo [%date% %time%] Run script started >> "%LOG%"

title karim ERP - Running

echo.

echo   karim ERP v3.0.0 - Starting
echo ============================================================

node scripts/start.js
set "EXIT_CODE=%errorlevel%"
echo [%time%] start.js exited with code %EXIT_CODE% >> "%LOG%"

echo.
echo ============================================================
if "%EXIT_CODE%"=="0" (
    echo   System stopped normally.
) else (
    echo   System stopped with exit code: %EXIT_CODE%
    echo.
    echo   If the system crashed, check:
    echo     1. Is another instance running? Run Stop in the launcher.
    echo     2. Is port 5000 in use? Run: netstat -ano ^| findstr :5000
    echo     3. Run Setup to verify everything is configured.
    echo.
    echo   Log file: %LOG%
)
echo ============================================================
echo.
echo Press any key to close.
pause >nul
exit /b %EXIT_CODE%
./karim-master.ps1
# ============================================================
#  🦅 karim ERP v2.1.0 — Windows Master Script (PowerShell)
#  ============================================================
#  سكربت موحد لكل عمليات karim ERP على ويندوز:
#    - setup:    تثبيت + تنزيل PG + migrations
#    - run:      تشغيل النظام
#    - test:     تشغيل الاختبارات
#    - build:    بناء .exe
#    - status:   فحص حالة الخادم
#    - stop:     إيقاف الخادم
#    - clean:    تنظيف node_modules + release
#    - reset-db: إعادة تهيئة قاعدة البيانات
#    - backup:   نسخة احتياطية من DB
#    - restore:  استعادة نسخة احتياطية
#    - all:      setup + run
#
#  الاستخدام:
#    powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File karim-master.ps1 setup
#    powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File karim-master.ps1 run
#    powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File karim-master.ps1 test
#    powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File karim-master.ps1 build nsis
#    powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File karim-master.ps1 all
# ============================================================

param(
    [Parameter(Position=0)]
    [ValidateSet('setup','run','test','build','status','stop','clean','reset-db','backup','restore','all','help','')]
    [string]$Command = 'help',

    [Parameter(Position=1)]
    [string]$SubCommand = ''
)

$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$AppVersion = '2.1.0'
$AppName = 'karim ERP'

# ─── Helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function Write-Header {
    param([string]$Title)
    Write-Host ''
    Write-Host ('=' * 60) -ForegroundColor DarkYellow
    Write-Host "  $Title" -ForegroundColor Yellow
    Write-Host ('=' * 60) -ForegroundColor DarkYellow
    Write-Host ''
}

function Write-Step {
    param([string]$Msg)
    Write-Host "  > $Msg" -ForegroundColor Cyan
}

function Write-OK {
    param([string]$Msg)
    Write-Host "  [OK] $Msg" -ForegroundColor Green
}

function Write-Err {
    param([string]$Msg)
    Write-Host "  [ERROR] $Msg" -ForegroundColor Red
}

function Test-Node {
    try {
        $v = node --version 2>$null
        if ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0) {
            return $true, $v
        }
    } catch {}
    return $false, $null
}

function Test-PostgresRunning {
    try {
        $result = Test-NetConnection -ComputerName localhost -Port 5432 -WarningAction SilentlyContinue
        return $result.TcpTestSucceeded
    } catch {
        return $false
    }
}

function Stop-BackendProcess {
    Write-Step 'Stopping any running karim ERP backend processes...'
    Get-Process -Name 'node' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Where-Object {
        $_.CommandLine -match 'backend[\\/]server\.js'
    } | ForEach-Object {
        Stop-Process -Id $_.Id -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
        Write-Host "    Stopped PID $($_.Id)" -ForegroundColor Gray
    }
    Get-Process -Name 'postgres' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Where-Object {
        $_.Path -match 'database[\\/]bin'
    } | ForEach-Object {
        Stop-Process -Id $_.Id -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
        Write-Host "    Stopped PostgreSQL PID $($_.Id)" -ForegroundColor Gray
    }
}

# ─── Commands ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

function Invoke-Setup {
    Write-Header "$AppName v$AppVersion - Setup"

    # Check Node
    Write-Step 'Checking Node.js...'
    $nodeOk, $nodeVer = Test-Node
    if (-not $nodeOk) {
        Write-Err 'Node.js is not installed. Install from https://nodejs.org/'
        exit 1
    }
    Write-OK "Node.js $nodeVer"

    # Check npm
    Write-Step 'Checking npm...'
    try {
        $npmVer = npm --version 2>$null
        if ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0) {
            Write-OK "npm $npmVer"
        } else { throw }
    } catch {
        Write-Err 'npm not found. Reinstall Node.js.'
        exit 1
    }

    # Install npm dependencies
    Write-Step 'Installing npm dependencies...'
    npm install --no-audit --no-fund
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        Write-Err 'npm install failed.'
        exit 1
    }
    Write-OK 'Dependencies installed'

    # Download PostgreSQL
    Write-Step 'Setting up PostgreSQL Portable...'
    npm run download-pg
    Write-OK 'PostgreSQL setup complete'

    # patch-html (no-op if legacy file missing)
    Write-Step 'Running patch-html compatibility check...'
    npm run patch-html
    Write-OK 'patch-html done'

    # Create .env
    Write-Step 'Creating .env file with strong random secrets...'
    if (Test-Path .env) {
        Write-Host '    .env already exists, skipping.' -ForegroundColor Gray
    } else {
        if (-not (Test-Path .env.example)) {
            Write-Err '.env.example not found.'
            exit 1
        }
        Copy-Item .env.example .env
        $accessSecret = node -e "console.log(require('crypto').randomBytes(48).toString('hex'))"
        $refreshSecret = node -e "console.log(require('crypto').randomBytes(48).toString('hex'))"
        (Get-Content .env) -replace '^JWT_ACCESS_SECRET=.*', "JWT_ACCESS_SECRET=$accessSecret" | Set-Content .env
        (Get-Content .env) -replace '^JWT_REFRESH_SECRET=.*', "JWT_REFRESH_SECRET=$refreshSecret" | Set-Content .env
        Write-OK '.env created with strong random secrets'
    }

    # Final syntax check
    Write-Step 'Verifying JS syntax across all files...'
    node -e "const {execSync}=require('child_process');const fs=require('fs');const path=require('path');function walk(d){let r=[];for(const f of fs.readdirSync(d)){const p=path.join(d,f);const s=fs.statSync(p);if(s.isDirectory())r=r.concat(walk(p));else if(f.endsWith('.js'))r.push(p);}return r;}let all=[];for(const d of ['backend','scripts','database/scripts','electron','tests','frontend/app/js','build']){if(fs.existsSync(d))all=all.concat(walk(d));}let err=0;for(const f of all){try{execSync('node --check \"'+f+'\"',{stdio:'pipe'});}catch(e){err++;console.log('FAIL:',f);}}console.log('Checked:',all.length,'files | Errors:',err);if(err>0)process.exit(1);"
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        Write-Err 'Syntax check failed.'
        exit 1
    }
    Write-OK 'All JS files have valid syntax'

    Write-Header 'Setup Complete!'
    Write-Host '  Next steps:' -ForegroundColor White
    Write-Host '    Start the system:   .\run-windows.bat   or   karim-master.ps1 run' -ForegroundColor Gray
    Write-Host '    Run tests:          .\test-windows.bat  or   karim-master.ps1 test' -ForegroundColor Gray
    Write-Host '    Build .exe:         .\build-windows.bat or   karim-master.ps1 build' -ForegroundColor Gray
    Write-Host ''
    Write-Host '  Default login: admin / 1234' -ForegroundColor Yellow
    Write-Host ''
}

function Invoke-Run {
    Write-Header "$AppName v$AppVersion - Starting"

    # Check .env
    if (-not (Test-Path .env)) {
        Write-Step '.env not found. Creating from .env.example...'
        if (Test-Path .env.example) {
            Copy-Item .env.example .env
            $accessSecret = node -e "console.log(require('crypto').randomBytes(48).toString('hex'))"
            $refreshSecret = node -e "console.log(require('crypto').randomBytes(48).toString('hex'))"
            (Get-Content .env) -replace '^JWT_ACCESS_SECRET=.*', "JWT_ACCESS_SECRET=$accessSecret" | Set-Content .env
            (Get-Content .env) -replace '^JWT_REFRESH_SECRET=.*', "JWT_REFRESH_SECRET=$refreshSecret" | Set-Content .env
            Write-OK '.env created'
        } else {
            Write-Err '.env.example not found. Run setup first.'
            exit 1
        }
    }

    # Check node_modules
    if (-not (Test-Path node_modules)) {
        Write-Step 'node_modules not found. Installing dependencies...'
        npm install --no-audit --no-fund
        if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
            Write-Err 'npm install failed.'
            exit 1
        }
    }

    Write-Step 'Starting karim ERP...'
    Write-Host ''
    Write-Host '  This will:' -ForegroundColor Gray
    Write-Host '    - Start PostgreSQL Portable' -ForegroundColor Gray
    Write-Host '    - Apply database migrations' -ForegroundColor Gray
    Write-Host '    - Start the Express server on port 5000' -ForegroundColor Gray
    Write-Host '    - Open your browser automatically' -ForegroundColor Gray
    Write-Host ''
    Write-Host '  Press Ctrl+C to stop safely.' -ForegroundColor Yellow
    Write-Host ''

    node scripts/start.js
}

./build/Start-Karim-ERP.bat
@echo off
chcp 65001 >nul 2>&1
title 🦅 karim ERP - Restaurant Management System

echo.
echo  ╔══════════════════════════════════════════╗
echo  ║                                          ║
echo  ║     🦅  KARIM ERP  🦅                    ║
echo  ║     Restaurant Management System         ║
echo  ║                                          ║
echo  ║     Powered by Ancient Egyptian          ║
echo  ║     Excellence                          ║
echo  ║                                          ║
echo  ╚══════════════════════════════════════════╝
echo.
echo  Starting system, please wait...
echo.

REM Set working directory to script location
cd /d "%~dp0"

REM Create .env from example if not exists
if not exist ".env" (
    if exist ".env.example" (
        copy ".env.example" ".env" >nul 2>&1
        echo  [OK] Created default configuration file.
    )
)

REM Check if node.exe exists
if not exist "%~dp0node.exe" (
    echo.
    echo  [ERROR] Node.js runtime not found!
    echo  Please reinstall karim ERP.
    echo.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)

REM Check if icon exists (visual confirmation)
if exist "%~dp0resources\icon.ico" (
    echo  [OK] karim ERP icon found.
)

echo.
echo  [INFO] Starting PostgreSQL database...
echo  [INFO] Starting Express server on port 5000...
echo.
echo  [!!] To stop: Close this window or press Ctrl+C
echo.

"%~dp0node.exe" "%~dp0scripts\start.js"

echo.
echo  🦅 karim ERP stopped.
pause
./Karim-ERP-Launcher.bat
@echo off
setlocal EnableDelayedExpansion EnableExtensions

:: ============================================================
::  karim ERP v3.0.0 - Robust Launcher
::  Never closes on error. Always logs + pauses.
:: ============================================================

cd /d "%~dp0"
chcp 65001 >nul 2>&1

set "LOG=%TEMP%\karim-launcher.log"
echo. > "%LOG%" 2>nul
echo [%date% %time%] Launcher started >> "%LOG%"
echo Working dir: %CD% >> "%LOG%"

title karim ERP v3.0.0 - Launcher

:: --- Pre-flight checks ---
call :preflight
if errorlevel 1 (
    echo.
    echo ============================================================
    echo   PRE-FLIGHT CHECK FAILED
    echo   See log: %LOG%
    echo ============================================================
    echo.
    pause
    goto menu
)

:menu
cls
echo.
echo ============================================================
echo   karim ERP v3.0.0 - Launcher
echo ============================================================
echo.
echo   [1] Setup      - First-time install (PG + deps + migrations)
echo   [2] Run        - Start the full system
echo   [3] Stop       - Stop backend + PostgreSQL
echo   [4] Status     - Check what's running
echo   [5] Test       - Run all tests
echo   [6] Backup     - Create database backup
echo   [7] Restore    - Restore from backup
echo   [8] Reset DB   - Delete and recreate database (DANGEROUS)
echo   [9] Safe Mode  - Backend only (no PG auto-start)
echo   [L] View Log   - Open launcher log
echo   [0] Exit
echo.
set "choice="
set /p choice="Enter your choice (0-9, L): "

if "!choice!"=="1" goto setup
if "!choice!"=="2" goto run
if "!choice!"=="3" goto stop
if "!choice!"=="4" goto status
if "!choice!"=="5" goto test
if "!choice!"=="6" goto backup
if "!choice!"=="7" goto restore
if "!choice!"=="8" goto resetdb
if "!choice!"=="9" goto safemode
if /i "!choice!"=="L" goto viewlog
if "!choice!"=="0" goto end

echo.
echo Invalid choice: "!choice!"
timeout /t 2 >nul
goto menu

:preflight
echo [PREFLIGHT] Checking environment... >> "%LOG%"
where node >nul 2>&1
if errorlevel 1 (
    echo [PREFLIGHT] FAIL: Node.js not found >> "%LOG%"
    echo.
    echo ERROR: Node.js is not installed or not in PATH.
    echo Install Node.js 18+ from: https://nodejs.org/
    exit /b 1
)
for /f "tokens=*" %%v in ('node --version 2^>nul') do set "NODE_VER=%%v"
echo [PREFLIGHT] Node.js: !NODE_VER! >> "%LOG%"
where npm >nul 2>&1
if errorlevel 1 (
    echo [PREFLIGHT] FAIL: npm not found >> "%LOG%"
    echo ERROR: npm is not installed.
    exit /b 1
)
echo test > .write-test 2>nul
if errorlevel 1 (
    echo [PREFLIGHT] FAIL: cannot write to current directory >> "%LOG%"
    echo ERROR: Cannot write to: %CD%
    exit /b 1
)
del .write-test >nul 2>&1
echo [PREFLIGHT] All checks passed >> "%LOG%"
exit /b 0

:setup
echo [%time%] Choice: setup >> "%LOG%"
echo.
node scripts/setup.js
echo [%time%] Setup returned exit code %errorlevel% >> "%LOG%"
goto back

:run
echo [%time%] Choice: run >> "%LOG%"
echo.
node scripts/start.js
echo [%time%] Run returned exit code %errorlevel% >> "%LOG%"
goto back

:stop
echo [%time%] Choice: stop >> "%LOG%"
node scripts/stop.js
goto back

:status
echo [%time%] Choice: status >> "%LOG%"
node scripts/status.js
goto back

:test
echo [%time%] Choice: test >> "%LOG%"
node scripts/test.js
goto back

:backup
echo [%time%] Choice: backup >> "%LOG%"
node database/scripts/backup.js
goto back

:restore
echo [%time%] Choice: restore >> "%LOG%"
echo.
echo Available backups:
dir /b database\backups\*.sql 2>nul
echo.
set "bkfile="
set /p bkfile="Enter backup filename (or full path): "
if "!bkfile!"=="" goto back
if exist "!bkfile!" (
    node database/scripts/restore.js "!bkfile!"
) else if exist "database\backups\!bkfile!" (
    node database/scripts/restore.js "database\backups\!bkfile!"
) else (
    echo File not found: !bkfile!
)
goto back

:resetdb
echo [%time%] Choice: resetdb >> "%LOG%"
echo.
echo WARNING: This will DELETE all data and recreate the database.
node scripts/reset.js
goto back

:safemode
echo [%time%] Choice: safemode >> "%LOG%"
echo.
if not exist .env (
    echo ERROR: .env not found. Run Setup first.
    goto back
)
if not exist node_modules (
    echo ERROR: node_modules not found. Run Setup first.
    goto back
)
echo Starting backend only (no PG auto-start)...
echo Press Ctrl+C to stop.
echo.
node backend/server.js
goto back

:viewlog
echo [%time%] Choice: viewlog >> "%LOG%"
if exist "%LOG%" notepad "%LOG%"
goto menu

:back
echo.
echo ------------------------------------------------------------
echo   Operation completed. Press any key to return to menu.
echo ------------------------------------------------------------
pause >nul
goto menu

:end
echo [%time%] Launcher exited >> "%LOG%"
exit /b 0
./setup-windows.bat
@echo off
setlocal EnableDelayedExpansion EnableExtensions

:: ============================================================
::  karim ERP v3.0.0 - Setup Script
::  Runs: npm install + PG download + init + migrations + .env
:: ============================================================

cd /d "%~dp0"
chcp 65001 >nul 2>&1

set "LOG=%TEMP%\karim-setup.log"
echo. > "%LOG%" 2>nul
echo [%date% %time%] Setup started >> "%LOG%"

title karim ERP - Setup

echo.
echo ============================================================
echo   karim ERP v3.0.0 - Setup
echo ============================================================
echo.

node scripts/setup.js
set "EXIT_CODE=%errorlevel%"
echo [%time%] setup.js exited with code %EXIT_CODE% >> "%LOG%"

echo.
if "%EXIT_CODE%"=="0" (
    echo ============================================================
    echo   Setup Complete!
    echo ============================================================
    echo.
    echo Next: Double-click run-windows.bat
    echo       Or: Run the launcher and choose [2] Run
    echo.
    echo Default login: admin / 1234
) else (
    echo ============================================================
    echo   Setup FAILED (exit code %EXIT_CODE%)
    echo ============================================================
    echo.
    echo Check the log: %LOG%
    echo Common fixes:
    echo   - Ensure internet connection (for npm + PG download)
    echo   - Run as Administrator if permission errors
    echo   - Disable antivirus temporarily
)
echo.
pause
exit /b %EXIT_CODE%
